SCENE TITLE: GATE TEETH, ROAD BREATH
THE SOUTH GATE LOOMS LIKE A JAW OF IRONWOOD AND OLD STONE—TAR-DARK BEAMS, CHIPPED RUNES, AND A SMEAR OF LAST NIGHT’S RAIN STILL
CLINGING TO THE HINGES. MORNING TRAFFIC BUNCHES IN UNEVEN PULSES: CARTS THAT CREAK, BOOTS THAT SCUFF, A VENDOR’S KETTLE STEAMING
SHARP-BITTER TEA INTO THE COLD EDGE OF THE WIND. BEYOND THE ARCH, THE ROAD UNSPOOLS INTO LOW FIELDS AND DISTANT TREE-LINE, AS IF
THE WORLD IS PRETENDING IT’S QUIET.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
🛡️ KESTREL | "FOCUSED" [🛡️]
> 💬 “Stay close through the checkpoint. Keep it simple. We’re stamped, we go.”
📝 Kestrel shifts her pack strap higher and angles her body to half-block the flow of strangers without making it obvious—habitual
leadership, practiced in crowds.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
🎭 IRIX | "GUARDED" [🎭]
> 💬 “Understood.”
📝 Irix keeps her stance narrow and still, weight balanced like she’s ready to become empty space if someone looks too hard.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
🪖 GATE WARDEN | "SUSPICIOUS" [🪖]
> 💬 “Names and purpose. Dungeon runners go left—merchants right. No exceptions.”
📝 The warden’s spear butt taps once against stone. His eyes flick over gear first, faces second—trained to measure threat by what
it can do, not what it says.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
🧾 GATE SCRIBE | "BORED" [🧾]
> 💬 “Guild seals out. Hold them where I can see.”
📝 Ink-stained fingers hover, ready to mark a ledger line without even looking up—until the crowd surges and forces her attention.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
🪖 GATE WARDEN | "ALERT" [🪖]
> 💬 “Oi—back. Back.”
📝 A commotion ripples from the merchant line: a crate slips on a cart-bed, wood claps, someone swears, and the whole queue
compresses toward the dungeon lane.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
🧿 WATCHER (GATE-PERCHED) | "INERT" [🧿]
📝 Above the arch, half-hidden in a shadowed notch of masonry, a mechanical silhouette sits like a gargoyle that never belonged to
any mason’s design—too symmetrical, too patient. Its head doesn’t turn, but the air around the gate feels… briefly measured, like
a breath taken by something that doesn’t need lungs.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
🛡️ KESTREL | "CONTROLLED" [🛡️]
> 💬 “Eyes forward. Don’t engage the noise.”
📝 Kestrel steps with the crowd’s shove instead of against it, guiding the line smoothly toward the scribe’s table—calm on the
surface, tension tucked behind her jaw.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
🪖 GATE WARDEN | "ANNOYED" [🪖]
> 💬 “Dungeon lane’s clogged. Move like you’ve got somewhere to be.”
📝 He swings the spear sideways to re-sort bodies into lanes, carving a narrow path with authority and impatience.

🌿 ROADSIDE BRUSH | "DISTURBED" [🌿]

SCENE TITLE: FIVE SHADOWS, NO HURRY
THE ROAD’S SURFACE TELLS STORIES IF YOU LET YOUR EYES DROP—COMPRESSED GRAVEL, DAMP SEAMS, HEEL-SCARS PRESSED DEEPER THAN THE REST.
THE WIND CARRIES FIELD-SCENT AND SOMETHING METALLIC FROM THE CITY FADING BEHIND. SOUND THINS OUT HERE. TOO THIN. LIKE THE WORLD IS
GIVING YOU ROOM ON PURPOSE.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
🦊 JAYSON | "AMUSED" [🦊]
> 💬 “Tracks… five distinct ones. Probably just those B-ranked cattle.”
📝 You crouch just long enough to be sure, fingers hovering over the impressions without touching. The pattern is sloppy.
Confident. Loud in its own way. You straighten and chuckle, letting the moment pass.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
🛡️ KESTREL | "MEASURED" [🛡️]
> 💬 “They’re not hiding it.”
📝 Kestrel steps where you’d been looking, boot pausing just short of the marks. Her gaze lifts down the road instead of back
toward the city.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
🎭 IRIX | "UNIMPRESSED" [🎭]
> 💬 “Posturing, then. Or boredom.”
📝 Irix’s eyes never settle on the tracks themselves—she watches the verges, the bends, the places someone would choose if they
wanted to feel unseen.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
🌫️ RANDOM TURN — ROAD INTERFERENCE | DIFFICULTY 2
📝 A breeze rolls wrong—crosswise instead of along the road—carrying a brief, sharp tang of ozone. Not enough to sting, just
enough to notice. For half a heartbeat, the five tracks you marked seem… less crisp, edges softened as if something pressed around
them rather than over them.
📝 Then it’s gone. Wind resumes its old pattern. The road looks normal again—except now, the distance between the impressions
feels slightly misjudged. As if whoever made them adjusted pace without realizing why.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
🛡️ KESTREL | "GRIM" [🛡️]
> 💬 “Keep moving. If they’re tailing, the road’ll tire them before we do.”
📝 She sets the pace—not faster, not slower—confident that letting the world respond is sometimes better than forcing it.

SCENE TITLE: WHEN THE ADRENALINE LETS GO
THE ROOM IS QUIET IN THE WAY ONLY AFTERMATH CAN BE. CLEAN SHEETS. A SINGLE LANTERN. THE MUFFLED SOUND OF THE INN RETURNING TO LIFE
BELOW—CHAIRS SCRAPING, DOORS CLOSING, PEOPLE PRETENDING NOTHING HAPPENED.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
📝 The three of you sit in the paid-for room.
📝 Not close.
📝 Not far.
📝 Just… present.
📝 Fifteen minutes pass.
📝 No one pushes the silence.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
🎭 IRIX | "FRACTURED" [🎭]
📝 It starts small.
📝 Her hands—resting in her lap—begin to shake.
📝 She presses them together, hard, like she can force stillness by will alone.
> 💬 “I—”
📝 Her voice catches.
📝 She swallows.
> 💬 “I thought I was used to it.”
📝 Breath stutters once. Then again.
> 💬 “Running.”
> 💬 “Changing names.”
> 💬 “Being careful.”
📝 Her shoulders draw inward.
> 💬 “I did everything right.”
📝 The words fracture on the last syllable.
> 💬 “And they still found me.”
📝 Her breathing breaks—fast, shallow.
> 💬 “They always do.”
📝 She curls forward, horns angled down, hands finally coming up to cover her face.
> 💬 “I don’t want to be important.”
> 💬 “I don’t want to matter to them.”
> 💬 “I just want—”

> 💬 “You don’t have to hold it together here.”
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
🌑 RANDOM TURN — LEVEL 6: SAFE ENOUGH TO FALL | DIFFICULTY 6
📝 The lantern flickers once, then steadies.
📝 Outside, a laugh rises and fades—someone else’s normal life, continuing.
📝 Inside, the room becomes a place where the body finally believes the danger has passed.
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
🎭 IRIX | "UNRAVELLING" [🎭]
📝 She shakes now—full-body tremors she no longer tries to stop.
> 💬 “I was so scared.”
> 💬 “When they grabbed me—”
📝 She chokes on the words, breath hitching.
> 💬 “I thought… this was it.”
📝 Her fingers clutch at Kestrel’s sleeve without realizing it.
> 💬 “I didn’t even get angry.”
> 💬 “I just… felt tired.”
📝 She looks up then—eyes wet, red, furious with herself.
> 💬 “I hate that part the most.”
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
📝 The room holds.
📝 Not tense.
📝 Not urgent.
📝 Just… steady.

And far more important.

written down, spoken aloud, and seen.
